Preface
I have been thinking about what our world is since I was a child, constantly asking why, and wanting to know the truth. But I soon discovered that no one knows about this matter. Yes, it is so important. It is even the question of why we exist. No one can explain it from the most basic level. Since then, my whole life seems to be in search of this truth, and I have been stuck in thinking about this problem until now. But if you ask me today, what is the truth, I have to tell you. I don’t know. I am still far away from understanding the nature of the world. Even I have thought that the architectural concept of thinking itself cannot explain the world. For example, you can't measure the temperature with a speedometer, or see a person with your nose, but I still want to share with you my thoughts that I have been thinking about so far, because I know there are countless people who want to know about this matter, even more crazy than me, but everyone has blind spots in thinking. I hope my article can inspire you or take a few steps forward. Maybe this generation can't understand it, but I believe that time is long, maybe a hundred years later, maybe a thousand years later, there will be people who can empathize with me, so it can also be said that this is a letter that spans space and time.

Chapter 1
I want to directly enter the world itself. How to enter? Let’s start with the most primitive human senses. Regarding thinking, why do people think this way and that way? Some historians and biologists will say that the additional abilities evolved because of survival are because people are group animals and must communicate. But when asked, why did they not evolve into social forms like ants or bees or animals other than insects such as wolves and elephants? Instead, concepts such as language, religion, law, and country emerged? These concepts themselves that we can intuitively respond to are due to differences in the brain itself. No matter who is more complex, More streamlined, this is the difference itself, which causes us to have different behavioral structures. More basically, we can see that it is the genes themselves that cause the difference. We can even boldly infer that if the gene sequence of bees and ants, that is, the position order of the bases, is changed to be exactly the same as that of humans, then humans will be born and there will be no difference. The only difference is that the structure is different and the combination is different. Nuo wants to see the more basic matter, which is atoms. It can be said that the arrangement and combination create our world. Even the more microscopic particles are arranged and combined to form a more macroscopic structure. Thinking about this, I can’t help but wonder, what structure are the planets intended to form?

If everything is a structure, all behavior itself will only be within the structure and cannot be transcended. This depends on the composition of the structure. Maybe it is complexity, maybe it is feedback excellence. From the micro to the macro, they are atoms, molecules, compounds, cells, animals and plants, mountain plates, planets, galaxies, and galaxies. We can also find that between structures, whether they are of the same size or in front and back, there are intervals rather than close connections. This is a characteristic of matter itself that is worth thinking about. What is the interval for? And this interval is often an exponential volume. There is even a kind of smaller basic components for the sake of a larger structure, and the question of micro-macro correlation is reserved here. Is there a closer relationship between them than we know?

Back to the thinking itself, first of all, I want to gain a consensus before we can proceed. What are the basic laws of human society based on? Morals, laws, religious beliefs, preferences, biological instincts to feed and reproduce, and a sense of collective identity. What is the essence of what we call inner thoughts? Nowadays, people are eager to mainstream and truth these thoughts, called values, to cooperate with God or God or birth to bring power to all people. It is just based on the structure that all thoughts are the same. I don’t know if it is true to say this now. Maybe it is part of a larger structure. I am still a person in the fog, but please let me explain the essence of these thoughts. I will ask you, what is good and evil? What is good and bad? From the public's perspective, good and bad seem to be better defined, such as good and bad things. It is a good thing to become a symbol of auspiciousness. This can be divided into two parts. One is the meaning of festivals such as the Spring Festival. The other is the difference in slaughtering different creatures. What are festivals? The Chinese Valentine's Day commemorates the Cowherd and the Weaver Girl. The Dragon Boat Festival commemorates Qu Yuan. Christmas commemorates Jesus. Another origin of the Spring Festival and the Dragon Boat Festival is to pray for blessings and ward off evil spirits. This means that when commemorating things or reaching a consensus, killing becomes a good deed. Then today, when everyone has a consensus, can we kill people at will? My purpose is to explore the second point of biological differences. From a biological point of view, humans and animals are made of proteins, both composed of the arrangement and growth of base genes. You may say that humans can think and invent. They are different from other creatures and are unique. I don’t deny that maybe the world’s truth standards are these, but the premise is that we need to confirm. In other areas, humans are not unique at all. Their strength is not comparable to that of most animals of the same size, and their speed is also inferior. Their sense of smell and hearing are even worse. Many insects can see colors that we cannot perceive, and all creatures are afraid of death. But only humans can control life and death so efficiently. The key to grasping the key to death lies precisely in the brain, where human beings develop best. Of course, there may be more powerful ability structures, but it does not exist in today's earth society. That's why humans are the overlords of the earth. In this case, if we can agree on whether we can eat or not, there is no uniqueness. They are all proteins and can be absorbed. There are many incidents of humans being eaten by animals. They only show that in areas that humans are not good at, humans must also abide by animal laws. I say this not to deny whether moral good and evil should exist, but whether they are the truth of the world. Human beings are good at using their brains, which is to make good use of everything given by their brains. This is in line with the laws of the world, but there is no prohibition against killing humans. This is also one of the laws. Some people will say that based on civilization, we should move in a specific direction. Slaughter should be abandoned. Next, we will discuss the nature of civilization.

Energy is the foundation of everything. If it is stagnant and hoarded, funds and investments will be unbalanced and collapse, which is the destruction of the stable triangle. Because the rule is that one of the three parties must grow, and the other party must grow to maintain the unstable triangle. If only one party grows and the other two parties do not grow, it will become a broken state that does not constitute a triangle. That is, one party exceeds the boundary. The other party must immediately grow to reach the unstable triangle first, and then slowly develop the third party to reach the stable triangle. This period is the stage of the development phase that becomes stable. After the breakthrough, it will collapse towards imbalance. If the other two parties do not develop, it will lead to the stage of economic regression. This is because it is in line with the scope of divergence of the thinking structure rather than the scope of diffusion and application of physical objects. This is also the weighting mechanism that I need to write in when establishing rules later. The three basic conditions for its balance point are the size of power. The scope of ideological influence and the degree of resource restriction. The degree of resource allocation is based on the living expenses of the individual's class. For example, those with high resources are assigned to the level with high food and beverage expenses. That is, the voting weight is also lower. The reason why they still want to stay at this level is that they have a high degree of ownership of resources, namely money, and are restricted through an open and transparent transaction system to prevent cross-level bribery to obtain power. That is, the positive triangle and inverted triangle mechanisms coexist and restrain them.

Why are modern mental illnesses such as depression dozens of times more common than in the past Internet era? I think it is due to the reason of relative comparison. In the past, when information was underdeveloped, the comparison objects were all based on the same type of growth environment. Because the world, including human thoughts, all position themselves based on relative comparative concepts. Therefore, today with the Internet, people can see the most beautiful and complete and successful life and growth forms of the world society, so they bring them into their own growth situation. This leads to the inward inferiority mentality of being unable to accept themselves and their families. As a result, inner mental illness follows. In the past, children could use the same similar experiences of friends and classmates around them to comfort themselves and relieve themselves. No one is better, but the current thinking structure has transitioned to other complex architecture models. The past architectural method of maintaining balance should be abandoned, which will inevitably lead to imbalance. Instead, with the changes of the times, this is a certain and unchangeable condition, to change the conditions that can adjust and cooperate, that is, the family structure thinking. We should follow the generally advocated methods and mentality of the Internet to educate and inherit future generations. Do not think that we should return to the past educational structure. Because the current Internet type has already become a dimension-enhancing complexity structure, it can be discussed and stacked endlessly to find out the perfect growth method and environmental mentality with the best personality. It should be the education method that changes together, rather than saying that we have been here like this before, because the growth environment is already in a backward dimension. If it should be discarded, don’t remember it anymore, and use it as a weapon to threaten the control section.

A token system based on the transfer of energy output and exchange between groups. Is there a mechanism that can get rid of the control of national currency issuance. The country and society can transfer and use this alternative currency even when it is in collapse. It must have a fixed sample frame that can be directly copied and used. The rule code can be discarded at any time and replaced with an architectural model that is more in line with the thinking of the next generation. The model uses the output party and the recipient as direct contract transactions. The initial currency can be purchased in the form of a stable currency and its daily growth amount is determined as its stable deflation and inflation ratio. If you want to directly use the mechanism to generate tokens The most basic way is to convert the energy mode, that is, the consumption level of electricity is linked to the number of tokens generated. The value of the electricity meter and the power company can be directly linked so that the user pays the resource that generates the energy in cash. This cash is used to purchase the generated tokens, and the tokens are returned to the individual worker who produces the energy-constructed items. Finally, when the goods are delivered from the hands of the worker to the end of the receiving user, the payment is made to the worker and the means of production. If the output of electricity cannot be used, the output of the token is directly linked to the quantity of tokens. Is it possible to objectively use a recognized credit system to determine the exchange quantity and proportion from other angles? For example, in the smart contract method, it is first agreed upon how much electricity can be exchanged for how many tokens. After the smart contract transaction is successful and both parties have reached a certain transaction number, the contract is completed and returned to the production token mechanism. The tokens are produced through the voting compliance mechanism of all managers. Since then, an objective and recognized conversion of electric energy and tokens has been formed, without subjective unlimited token issuance events and decoupling behaviors, and the produced tokens enter the circulation system. Initially, the energy generator, the national system, obtains tokens and then spends them to hire labor to produce goods and pay tokens as remuneration. This cycle repeats. The currency issued by the state must be destroyed and cannot be issued again until it is completely replaced by tokens. Since then, the complete linkage between energy and tokens has been completed.

The balance and stability generated through self-development are still in the current stage of development as a mechanism to test whether it can run for a long time. During this testing period, we can collect countless balances and developments beyond the understanding of the original structure, and then produce a balance model that may replace the past millennium, that is, after social saturation, it is overthrown and goes to extremes through war and killing. The cycle continues. This is the only and stable social form that human beings can construct for thousands of years. Today, the possibility of seeing other structures replaced is born. If we can carry it forward and use it, can we break out of this thousand-year architectural cycle curse? And the first thing I do is from this encrypted decentralized interaction model, as a bystander, to see clearly the purest and most instinctive direction of interaction between people without the involvement of a third party, that is, the direction of the selected mode of thinking. If there is a correction mechanism for self-stable balance. can confirm its real existence and the feasibility of replacing the current social model

Is there a technology with dimensional directionality that can threaten high-dimensional existence? And I think the so-called myth is not a record of the past, but more like a prophecy of the future. And this complexity corresponds to the development of artificial intelligence. If it continues to develop, it will mean that mankind as a whole continues to build the Tower of Babel. The distance to usher in divine punishment is getting closer and closer. Why is it said to be a prophecy of the future? Because through history, we will find that the same event structure is repeated over and over again. This is the micro direction field, and myth corresponds to the macro direction field. And our own thinking is equivalent to this macro structure. Other human beings have the same proportion to society's repeated destruction and reset. That is, all my thoughts are just the repetition of greater reincarnations. It’s just that its span may be a mythical interval, while the span of other social contributors is an era interval. Therefore, this mythical reincarnation will become inevitable, that is, the Tower of Babel will inevitably appear again and mankind will be divided and reset again. Because if human beings really reach the next interval of complexity, that is, existence on the same level as gods, would you accept it if they were them? It means that the reason why low-dimensional beings are in low dimensions is intentional. But if my idea is true, why would God let me exist, and why do gods need to attack humans? Does it mean that regarding directionality, humans and gods are equal beings? It’s just the amount of complexity that determines who is in the low dimension and who is in the high dimension. That is to say, a war between humans and gods will inevitably happen, and one side will be led by thinkers who can sense my conclusion. They will use the directionality of the complexity architecture as a weapon to see which side can win first.

Is language the remaining form of communication after human beings are deprived of their ability to communicate internally, which leads to different languages and the development of different cultures in different regions? If it were in the era of connected hearts, I believe its development speed would be exponentially faster than it is now, because as long as one person understands or discovers any new conceptual structure, it can be communicated directly and made known to everyone. Today's language society has restored this form of communication through other means, that is, the Internet and a large amount of information bombardment, through endless repetition of information content and discussion from different angles, in-depth understanding. The superposition speed of its diversity and complexity is no less than the use of the ability to communicate with the soul to stack civilization, that is, the completeness and comprehensiveness of complexity, and languages are gradually unifying like the globalization of English. The biggest problem now is that no one has come forward to discover or describe the existence of this dimension of directional complexity. Instead, directly open up the complexity of all the latest theories and thinking structures so that individual children can change and absorb them according to their own growth speed, rather than the modern collective indoctrination and teaching of the same views and social rules. This is an unchanging convergence behavior for the next generation regarding the future development speed and social structure. Divergent education and thinking teaching are the construction methods and individual states that should belong to the overall society in the future. There is never a so-called mature body and juvenile body. This is continuous change rather than different types. Therefore, education seems to only affect children. In fact, it is a new architectural communication model for the entire social state. If it is based on divergence, it will have enough stability and sustainability to allow unlimited social types to exist at the same time. Use architecture to explore architecture, so that more directional architectures can exist in the world at the same time.
